1\n121\t    lfsrState15Mask = int(math.pow(2, 15))\n122\t    lfsrState13Mask = int(math.pow(2, 13))\n123\t    lfsrState12Mask = int(math.pow(2, 12))\n124\t    lfsrState10Mask = int(math.pow(2, 10))\n125\t\n126\t    # Current lfsr state\n127\t    lfsrState = seed;\n128\t\n129\t    # Check 65534 cycles for correct output (the last output, 65535, should be the seed again)\n130\t    for i in range(0,65534) :\n131\t        lfsrState15 = (lfsrState & lfsrState15Mask) >> 15\n132\t        lfsrState13 = (lfsrState & lfsrState13Mask) >> 13\n133\t        lfsrState12 = (lfsrState & lfsrState12Mask) >> 12\n134\t        lfsrState10 = (lfsrState & lfsrState10Mask) >> 10\n135\t\n136\t        lfsrNextState0 = lfsrState15 ^ lfsrState13 ^ lfsrState12 ^ lfsrState10;\n137\t        lfsrNextState = (lfsrState << 1) & lfsrMask\n138\t        lfsrNextState = lfsrNextState | lfsrNextState0\n139\t        \n140\t        lfsrNextOut = (lfsrNextState & lfsrOutMask) >> 15\n141\t\n142\t        if i == 0:\n143\t            sinkBitsFile.write(str(lfsrNextOut) + \"_\" + bin16(lfsrNextState) + \\\n144\t                               \"        // Check each step for 65535 cycles\\n\")\n145\t        else :\n146\t            sinkBitsFile.write(str(lfsrNextOut) + \"_\" + bin16(lfsrNextState) + \"\\n\")\n147\t\n148\t        lfsrState = lfsrNextState\n149\t\n150\t    # Check the last cycle is the seed again\n151\t    sinkBitsFile.write(str(lfsrOutFirst) + \"_\" + bin16(seed) + \\\n152\t                       \"        // Check the last output is the seed again\\n\");\n153\t\n154\t    sinkBitsFile.close ()\n155\t\n156\tif __name__ == \"__main__\" :\n157\t    main ()\n158\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_015M4xGbSemEjv4QcFjfk3CY","session_id":"d2df4346-8e5e-4b50-8e26-4e70aa765c63","uuid":"0147d506-5f37-4f13-9bf7-55f892d4ca84","timestamp":"2026-05-05T07:51:31.482Z"}

the test benches.\n    // Thus, it is a reg.\n    reg [63:0] RANDOM_DELAY; \n\n    //\n    // Internal Parmeters\n    //\n    parameter WAIT_RANDOM    = 1'b0;\n    parameter PUSH_SEND_DATA = 1'b1;\n\n    //\n    // Signal Declarations\n    //\n\n    // Memory to hold test vectors\n    reg [BIT_WIDTH-1:0]     m_f[ENTRIES-1:0];\n\n    // Input index signals\n    reg [LOG2_ENTRIES-1:0]  index_f;\n    reg [LOG2_ENTRIES-1:0]  index_next;\n    reg                     index_en;\n\n    // Random delay signals\n    reg [31:0]              rand_delay_f;\n    reg [31:0]              rand_delay_next;\n    reg                     rand_delay_en;\n    reg                     rand_delay_init;\n    \n    // Output queue signals\n    reg [BIT_WIDTH-1:0]    outq_enq_bits;\n    reg                    outq_wr_en;\n    wire                   outq_full;\n\n    // State register\n    reg                    state_f;\n    reg                    state_next;\n\n    //\n    // Synchronous logic\n    //\n\n    // All flip-flops\n    always @ (posedge clk)\n    begin\n        if (~rst_n)\n        begin\n            index_f         <= 0;\n            rand_delay_f    <= 0;\n            state_f         <= WAIT_RANDOM;\n        end\n        else\n        begin\n            index_f         <= index_next;\n            rand_delay_f    <= rand_delay_next;\n            state_f         <= state_next;\n        end\n    end\n\n    //\n    // Combinational logic\n    //\n\n    // Entry index next value\n    always @ *\n    begin\n        index_next = index_f;\n        if (index_en)\n            index_next = index_f + 1;\n    end\n\n    // Random delay counter next value\n    always @ *\n    begin\n        rand_delay_next = rand_delay_f;\n        if (rand_delay_en)\n            rand_delay_next = rand_delay_f - 1;\n        else if (rand_delay_init)\n            rand_delay_next = {$random} % RANDOM_DELAY;\n    end\n\n    // Next state logic\n    always @ *\n    begin\n        state_next = state_f;\n        case (state_f)\n            WAIT_RANDOM:\n            begin\n                if (!outq_full && !done && (rand_delay_f == 0))\n                    state_next = PUSH_SEND_DATA;\n            end\n            PUSH_SEND_DATA:\n            begin\n                if (outq_full || done || (RANDOM_DELAY > 0))\n                begin\n                    state_next = WAIT_RANDOM;\n                end\n            end\n        endcase\n    end\n\n    // Enable bits\n    always @ *\n    begin\n        index_en        = (state_f == PUSH_SEND_DATA) & ~outq_full & ~done;\n        rand_delay_en   = (state_f == WAIT_RANDOM) & (rand_delay_f > 0) & ~done; \n        rand_delay_init = (state_f == PUSH_SEND_DATA) & (RANDOM_DELAY > 0) & ~done;\n    end\n\n    // Output queue signals\n    always @ *\n    begin\n        outq_wr_en      = (state_f == PUSH_SEND_DATA) & ~outq_full && ~done;\n        outq_enq_bits   = m_f[index_f];\n    end\n\n    // Done signal\n    always @ *\n        done = ((m_f[index_f] === {BIT_WIDTH{1'bx}}) | (index_f == (ENTRIES-1)));\n\n    //\n    // Helper module instantiations\n    //\n\n    // Output queue\n    test_infrstrct_fifo\n    #(\n        .BIT_WIDTH (BIT_WIDTH),\n        .ENTRIES (16),\n        .LOG2_ENTRIES (4)\n    ) outQ\n    (\n        .clk (clk),\n        .rst_n (rst_n),\n        .din (outq_enq_bits),\n        .wr_en (outq_wr_en),\n                                                  \\\n            $display (\"Simulation -> FAIL (HIT BAD TRAP)\");                         \\\n            $finish;                                                                \\\n        end                                                                         \\\n    endcase\n```\n\n## 2. /app/piton/verif/env/test_infrstrct/test_source.v\n\n```verilog\n// Copyright (c) 2015 Princeton University\n// All rights reserved.\n// \n// Redistribution and use in source and binary forms, with or without\n// modification, are permitted provided that the following conditions are met:\n//     * Redistributions of source code must retain the above copyright\n//       notice, this list of conditions and the following disclaimer.\n//     * Redistributions in binary form must reproduce the above copyright\n//       notice, this list of conditions and the following disclaimer in the\n//       documentation and/or other materials provided with the distribution.\n//     * Neither the name of Princeton University nor the\n//       names of its contributors may be used to endorse or promote products\n//       derived from this software without specific prior written permission.\n// \n// THIS SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED BY PRINCETON UNIVERSITY \"AS IS\" AND\n// ANY EXPRESS OR IMPLIED WARRANTIES, INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, THE IMPLIED\n// WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY AND FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE ARE\n// DISCLAIMED. IN NO EVENT SHALL PRINCETON UNIVERSITY BE LIABLE FOR ANY\n// DIRECT, INDIRECT, INCIDENTAL, SPECIAL, EXEMPLARY, OR CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES\n// (INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, PROCUREMENT OF SUBSTITUTE GOODS OR SERVICES;\n// LOSS OF USE, DATA, OR PROFITS; OR BUSINESS INTERRUPTION) HOWEVER CAUSED AND\n// ON ANY THEORY OF LIABILITY, WHETHER IN CONTRACT, STRICT LIABILITY, OR TORT\n// (INCLUDING NEGLIGENCE OR OTHERWISE) ARISING IN ANY WAY OUT OF THE USE OF THIS\n// SOFTWARE, EVEN IF ADVISED OF THE POSSIBILITY OF SUCH DAMAGE.\n\n/*\n * Contains a test source module for providing test vectors to\n * a module or set of modules. Test vectors are read from\n * a .vmh file\n */\n\nmodule test_source\n#(\n    parameter BIT_WIDTH         = 64,   // data width of stimuli\n    //parameter RANDOM_DELAY      = 0,    // random delay between test stimuli\n    parameter ENTRIES           = 1024, // number of test vector entries\n    parameter LOG2_ENTRIES      = 10    // log base 2 of the number of test vector entries\n)\n(\n    input                       clk,\n    input                       rst_n,\n\n    input                       rdy,\n\n    output [BIT_WIDTH-1:0]      bits,\n    output                      val,\n    output reg                  done\n);\n\n    // This is really a parameter, but VCS doesn't like\n    // parameters to be used in left hand sides of always @ *\n    // blocks like I want to use it for the test benches.\n    // Thus, it is a reg.\n    reg [63:0] RANDOM_DELAY; \n\n    //\n    // Internal Parmeters\n    //\n    parameter WAIT_RANDOM    = 1'b0;\n    parameter PUSH_SEND_DATA = 1'b1;\n\n    //\n    // Signal Declarations\n    //\n\n    // Memory to hold test vectors\n    reg [BIT_WIDTH-1:0]     m_f[ENTRIES-1:0];\n\n    // Input index signals\n    reg [LOG2_ENTRIES-1:0]  index_f;\n    reg [LOG2_ENTRIES-1:0]  index_next;\n    reg                     index_en;\n\n    // Random delay signals\n    reg [31:0]              rand_delay_f;\n    reg [31:0]              rand_delay_next;\n    reg                     rand_delay_en;\n    reg                     rand_delay_init;\n    \n    // Output queue signals\n    reg [BIT_WIDTH-1:0]    outq_enq_bits;\n    reg                    outq_wr_en;\n    wire                   outq_full;\n\n    // State register\n    reg                    state_f;\n    reg                    state_next;\n\n    //\n    // Synchronous logic\n    //\n\n    // All flip-flops\n    always @ (posedge clk)\n    begin\n        if (~rst_n)\n        begin\n            index_f         <= 0;\n            rand_delay_f    <= 0;\n            state_f         <= WAIT_RANDOM;\n        end\n        else\n        begin\n            index_f         <= index_next;\n            rand_delay_f    <= rand_delay_next;\n            state_f         <= state_next;\n        end\n    end\n\n    //\n    // Combinational logic\n    //\n\n    // Entry index next value\n    always @ *\n    begin\n        index_next = index_f;\n        if (index_en)\n            index_next = index_f + 1;\n    end\n\n    // Random delay counter next value\n    always @ *\n    begin\n        rand_delay_next = rand_delay_f;\n        if (rand_delay_en)\n            rand_delay_next = rand_delay_f - 1;\n        else if (rand_delay_init)\n            rand_delay_next = {$random} % RANDOM_DELAY;\n    end\n\n    // Next state logic\n    always @ *\n    begin\n        state_next = state_f;\n        case (state_f)\n            WAIT_RANDOM:\n            begin\n                if (!outq_full && !done && (rand_delay_f == 0))\n                    state_next = PUSH_SEND_DATA;\n            end\n            PUSH_SEND_DATA:\n            begin\n                if (outq_full || done || (RANDOM_DELAY > 0))\n                begin\n                    state_next = WAIT_RANDOM;\n                end\n            end\n        endcase\n    end\n\n    // Enable bits\n    always @ *\n    begin\n        index_en        = (state_f == PUSH_SEND_DATA) & ~outq_full & ~done;\n        rand_delay_en   = (state_f == WAIT_RANDOM) & (rand_delay_f > 0) & ~done; \n        rand_delay_init = (state_f == PUSH_SEND_DATA) & (RANDOM_DELAY > 0) & ~done;\n    end\n\n    // Output queue signals\n    always @ *\n    begin\n        outq_wr_en      = (state_f == PUSH_SEND_DATA) & ~outq_full && ~done;\n        outq_enq_bits   = m_f[index_f];\n    end\n\n    // Done signal\n    always @ *\n        done = ((m_f[index_f] === {BIT_WIDTH{1'bx}}) | (index_f == (ENTRIES-1)));\n\n    //\n    // Helper module instantiations\n    //\n\n    // Output queue\n    test_infrstrct_fifo\n    #(\n        .BIT_WIDTH (BIT_WIDTH),\n        .ENTRIES (16),\n        .LOG2_ENTRIES (4)\n    ) outQ\n    (\n        .clk (clk),\n        .rst_n (rst_n),\n        .din (outq_enq_bits),\n        .wr_en (outq_wr_en),\n        .rd_en (rdy),\n        .dout (bits),\n        .full (outq_full),\n        .empty (),\n        .val (val)\n    );\n\nendmodule\n```\n\n## 3.   rand_delay_init;\n\n    // Input queue signals\n    wire [BIT_WIDTH-1:0]    inq_deq_bits;\n    wire                    inq_full;\n    wire                    inq_empty;\n    reg                     inq_rdy;  \n    \n    // Fire signals\n    reg                     verify_fire;\n\n    // State register\n    reg                     state_f;\n    reg                     state_next;\n\n    //\n    // Synchronous Logic\n    //\n\n    // All flip-flops\n    always @ (posedge clk)\n    begin\n        if (~rst_n)\n        begin\n            index_f         <= 0;\n            rand_delay_f    <= 0;\n Ports:\n   - `clk`, `rst_n` (active-low reset)\n   - `lfsr_seed[15:0]`, `lfsr_ld`, `lfsr_step` for control\n   - `lfsr_state_f[15:0]` and `lfsr_out_f` for output\n\nLooking at the test cases, the behavior is clear: reset clears the state to zero, loading takes priority when `lfsr_ld` is asserted, stepping shifts the LFSR forward when `lfsr_step` is active, and the output `lfsr_out_f` reflects the MSB of the current state.\n\nThe key insight is that zero is a lock-up state—once the LFSR reaches all zeros, stepping keeps it there. The priority order in the control logic is: reset first, then load (which overrides step), then step if load isn't active, otherwise hold the state unchanged.\n\nNow I'm working through the polynomial x^16 + x^14 + x^13 + x^11 + 1 to determine the feedback taps. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-6","id":"msg_01Y9ZfiWZLVb1vmZqiLy9rRg","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"`110` = bit2=1, bit1=1, bit0=0 → step=0, clear=1, set=1\nThe sink shows all zeros → clear has priority over set.\n\nSo priority: clear > set > step.\n\nNow for the set operation itself, from step_set test (101): set=1, step=1 → count stays at 0x0001.\n\nAfter reset (count=0), with set=1 (regardless of step=0 or step=1):\n- count becomes 0x0001\n\nWait, but does `set` always set to exactly 0x0001? Let me think about why...\n\n\nIn the context of OpenPiton's ESL, this counter tracks speculation events and timeouts. The `set` signal might be setting a specific bit or value to mark a pending event. Setting to 0x0001 could indicate the counter just started or is in an active state. I'm wondering if `set` is performing a bitwise OR with 1 to set the lowest bit, or if it's setting the entire counter to a fixed value like 0x0001.\n\nAfter working through the priority logic, I think `set` simply means \"set counter to 1\", with a clear priority: clear (0) > set (1) > step (increment). {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-6","id":"msg_01WrujAqoiZAW7VPAi4Afhqc","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Now I understand the shift register behavior:\n\nSource format:\n- Bit 0: step\n- Bit 1: set\n\nSource: `10` = bit1=1, bit0=0 → step=0, set=1 (first entry: set)\nThen `01` = bit1=0, bit0=1 → step=1, set=0 (rest: step)\n\nSink output:\n- After reset: shift_reg_f = 0 (all zeros, 49 bits)\n- After set (but not yet applied): still 0 (one cycle latency)\n\n\n- After the first step, the bit shifts to position 0, then continues shifting left through positions 1-48 over the next 48 cycles\n- At cycle 50, the bit wraps back around to position 0 since it's a 49-bit circular shift register\n- The `set` operation loads 1 into bit 0, and each `step` rotates the bit left by one position, cycling back after reaching bit 48\n- The rotation works by taking bits 47 down to 0 and shifting them left, then wrapping bit 48 back to bit 0\n- Starting with bit 0 set to 1, after each step the 1 moves one position left, cycling back around after reaching bit 48\n\n.\n\nAfter 48 steps, bit48 cycles back to position 0, confirming the rotation logic works correctly.
